\"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\n\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The 500-step ISR of 0.123 was not a floor — it was a snapshot of ongoing collapse.\"}, \"plan018-additive-random-b01-s44-r2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"spec\": {\"model_name\": \"Qwen3-4B\", \"data_path\": \"data/train_500_seed42.jsonl\", \"data_count\": 500, \"seed\": 44, \"eval_data\": \"proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl\", \"conclusion_reason\": \"D095 negative: H7=0, H1=73 (82%), H8=16 (18%), genuine=6 (6.7%).
